======================================================================
OPERATING SYSTEMS KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Operating System Fundamentals
Concept: Operating System
Definition: System software that manages computer hardware and software resources.
Key Points:
- Fundamental operating system concept.
- Frequently asked in university exams and interviews.
- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.
Example:
Operating System is commonly encountered in modern operating systems.
Related Concepts:
Processes, Threads, Scheduling, Memory Management, File Systems, Synchronization

Entry 2
Category: Operating System Fundamentals

- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.

Entry 5
Category: Operating System Fundamentals
Concept: Booting
Definition: The process of starting a computer and loading the operating system.
Key Points:
- Fundamental operating system concept.
- Frequently asked in university exams and interviews.
- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.
Example:
BIOS loads the bootloader, which starts the OS.
Related Concepts:
Processes, Threads, Scheduling, Memory Management, File Systems, Synchronization

Entry 6
Category: Operating System Fundamentals
Concept: Interrupt
Definition: A signal that requests immediate CPU attention.
Key Points:
- Fundamental operating system concept.
- Frequently asked in university exams and interviews.
- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.
Example:
Interrupt is commonly encountered in modern operating systems.
Related Concepts:
Processes, Threads, Scheduling, Memory Management, File Systems, Synchronization

Entry 7
Category: Operating System Fundamentals
Concept: Process
Definition: A program in execution.
Key Points:
- Fundamental operating system concept.
- Frequently asked in university exams and interviews.
- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.
Example:
A web browser running on your computer.
Related Concepts:
Processes, Threads, Scheduling, Memory Management, File Systems, Synchronization

Entry 8
Category: Operating System Fundamentals
Concept: Thread
Definition: The smallest unit of CPU execution within a process.
Key Points:
- Fundamental operating system concept.
- Frequently asked in university exams and interviews.
- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.
Example:
Separate threads handle UI and downloads.
Related Concepts:
Processes, Threads, Scheduling, Memory Management, File Systems, Synchronization

Entry 19

- Fundamental operating system concept.
- Frequently asked in university exams and interviews.
- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.
Example:
Used to synchronize producer-consumer problems.
Related Concepts:
Processes, Threads, Scheduling, Memory Management, File Systems, Synchronization

Entry 20
Category: Process Management
Concept: Mutex
Definition: Ensures mutual exclusion for shared resources.
Key Points:
- Fundamental operating system concept.
- Frequently asked in university exams and interviews.
- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.
Example:
Only one thread can access shared data at a time.
Related Concepts:
Processes, Threads, Scheduling, Memory Management, File Systems, Synchronization

Category: Process Management
Concept: Semaphore
Definition: Synchronization primitive for resource management.
Key Points:
- Fundamental operating system concept.
- Frequently asked in university exams and interviews.
- Important for understanding system performance and resource management.
Example:
Used to synchronize producer-consumer problems.
Related Concepts:
Processes, Threads, Scheduling, Memory Management, File Systems, Synchronization
